3a1(1): @##// O //nu gang gi tshe yab 'das nas khyod rgyal por bcug pa de'i tshe /bdag la ci zhig bgyi//

8b1(1): gyur to  /de bas gsol ba ni gal te rgya zhig la nor zangs bdag tshol zhing mchis na/de la bdag gi sor gdub stsol la/'di skad ces kyang gsung ba'i rig so//gzhon nu lam yang

21b7(1): pa//'di ni su'i mthu 'dra ba//shin tu gsal bar shes par bya//de na gnas 'jog dang/rus gcig pa rgan po shu ba can ltas shes pa mkhas pa gzhan zhig yod pa des//

22a1(1): @#/ O /thub can ltas shes pa la /mkhas pa gzhan zhig yod pa des /drang srong de rnams don go bar byas pa/sa ni mi bzad g.yos gyur pa/'di ni su'i mthu ltas yin/
22a2(1): gang gi mthu ni 'di 'dra ba/shin tu gsal bar shes par bya/de nas 'jog dang rus gcig pa rgan thub can ltas shes pa la/mkhas (pa)gzhan zhig yod pa des / drang srong de rnams don de go bar byas/
22a3(1): gdon mi za bar dka' thub kyi gnas na 'dug pa'i bu byis pa 'bras dang/chu mthu ba yid du 'ong ba dang/mig du sdug par byed pa gnyis /thams cad sgrol gyis skye bo sdug bsngal gyi nyams 
22a4(1): thag pa yongs su thar bar bya ba'i phyir spangs te/de nas g.yos par gyur to//de nas byis pa gzhon nu de gnyis kyi phas snying nas yongs su btang ba'i blo rig nas/snying rje skad du ngu zhing 
22a5(1): thal mo gnyis sbyar nas/thams cad sgrol gyi rkang pa gnyis la btugs te smras pa/yab cig yum ni ma mchis par /khyod ni bdag cag stsogs bzhed pa//re shig de ni mthong gyur nas//

22b1(1): blo rig nas /pha'i rkang pa gnyis la phyag 'tshal te /thal mo sbyar nas snying rje rje skad du phab pa'i tshig gis dal gyis smras pa/gal te khyod kyis thag bkum nas/ma la bdag 
22b2(1): cag gnyis kyis phyin/khyod kyis bzod pa mdzod cig ces //yab cig khyod kyis gsung du gsol/yab gzhan yang byis par gyur pas bla na med pa khyod la nongs pa bgyis 
22b3(1): pa gang /gal te la lar thugs la mi 'bab tshig 'ga' smras pa 'aM//gus par mnyan par yongs su gnyes pa nyid du bgyis //de ni byis pa'i nongs pa lags zhes bzod pa mdzad

22b7(1): zha'i sgra byin cing /e ma 'o rab sbyin bdag nyid che/byis pa gnyis btang nas kyang /gang gi yid ni 'gyur med ba /de'i nges pa rmad gcig go/de nas rgyags sbyin ma rtsa ba dang //

23a1(1): @#/ O /sems dpa' thams cad thar bar bya bar brtson par byas pa'i sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i bar chad byed par 'gyur du 'ong ro snyam nas /seng ge mo zhig gis gzugs

23b1(1): te|phyogs 'di na ni gzhon nu nag po sring mo dang/lha na cig ri dgas kyi phru gu dang rtag tu rtse ste/'di ni gnyis sa rdul bas byas pa'i khyim yin 'di ni de gnyis kyi rtse mo las spyad
